## [2026-05-12] schema | CLAUDE.md — Part 6 coverage rule + body table + Part 18 lint check
Pages touched: [[CLAUDE]], [[lint/2026-05-11-ingredient-metal-coverage]], [[lint/index]], `tools/audit-ingredient-metal-coverage.mjs`
Notes: Closed the asymmetric-coverage failure mode in which ingredient-page ingests advanced one or two analytes (typically the source's primary metal) while leaving the remaining HMTc-relevant sub-blocks at template-default `pending`. Part 6 now mandates that any ingest touching an ingredient page must advance all ten `contamination_profile` sub-blocks out of `pending`; analytes the source does not address move to `populated, n_studies: 0` as declared data gaps. Part 6 also mandates a body-level "Heavy metal contamination profile" table (six columns, ten rows) as the stakeholder-facing surface, with Part 15 carving out a sanctioned exception to the multi-column-table guidance for this case. Part 18 adds two lint checks: ingredient coverage asymmetry and missing body table. Initial audit shows 110 mixed-state failures and 127 pages without the body table across 185 ingredient pages; cocoa and chocolate (which the prior session declared multi-metal-advanced) are among the worst offenders with 5 pending sub-blocks each.

## [2026-05-11] schema | cycle-33-gap-report-tooling-located-and-regenerated — Architectural finding on curator-vs-tooling layer divergence
Pages affected: data/evidence/hmtc_standards_gap_report.csv (regenerated; 233 rows), data/evidence/hmtc_standards_gap_summary.json (regenerated).
Notes: Cycle 33 of Tier-3a sweep. Located gap-report regeneration tooling: `tools/evidence/build-standards-gap-report.mjs`. Ran successfully.

  **Tooling input dependencies**:
   - `data/evidence/category1_formula_concentration_summary.csv`
   - `data/evidence/category1_fda_baby_food_compliance_summary.csv`
   - `data/evidence/category1_local_baby_food_occurrence_summary.csv`
   - `data/evidence/category5_plant_milk_occurrence_summary.csv`
   - `data/evidence/category5_tds_finished_food_occurrence_summary.csv`
   - `data/evidence/category5_apple_juice_arsenic_speciation_summary.csv`
   - `data/evidence/category5_grape_juice_inorganic_arsenic_summary.csv`
   - `data/evidence/product_regulatory_crosswalk.csv`
   - `data/evidence/local_reingest_queue.csv`
   - `data/evidence/local_reingest_candidate_values.csv`
   - `data/evidence/local_reingest_context_dispositions.csv`
   - `data/evidence/fda_tds_product_route_candidates.csv`
   - `wiki/products/*.md` (frontmatter only — `products`, `metals`, `evidence_tier`, etc.)

  **Important finding — curator-vs-tooling layer divergence**:

  Status distribution after regeneration (233 cells):
   - 88 BLOCKED: summary evidence only
   - 33 BLOCKED: no structured evidence loaded
   - 26 DO NOT PUBLISH DIRTY P10: single distribution-capable source
   - 19 DO NOT PUBLISH CLEAN P90: single distribution-capable source
   - 12 DO NOT PUBLISH INDEPENDENT P90: single distribution-capable source
   - 9 BLOCKED: evidence fitness review needed
   - 4 BLOCKED: documented local sources are context-only
   - 3 BLOCKED: species-specific evidence missing
   - **1 READY FOR AGGREGATE MATH REVIEW** (fruit-juice-not-canned iAs only)
   - 38 OUT OF SCOPE / CONTEXT ONLY

  This is **identical** to the pre-cycle-1 distribution, despite my 47 at-bar cells documented in the IandC master. The reason: the tooling has a STRICTER definition than my master-cell narrative:
   - Tooling "distribution-capable" = sample-level distribution source only (the source has sample-level CSV like FDA 2024 baby-food compliance)
   - Summary-level A-tier corroborators (Chekri 2019, FSA 2016, Kirkpatrick 1980, Elsheikh 2020, etc.) do NOT count toward tooling's distribution-capable count
   - "READY FOR AGGREGATE MATH REVIEW" requires 2+ distribution-capable sources

  **Reconciliation**:
   - Both views are correct under their respective definitions
   - **Master narrative** (curator-synthesis layer): cells at-bar at "Path A n_a_tier=N medium" per CLAUDE.md Part 6 (1-2 studies = low, 3+ = medium); summary-level + sample-level both count toward n_a_tier. This is what humans read.
   - **Gap report** (tooling-operational layer): cells "READY" require 2+ sample-level distribution-capable A-tier sources. Stricter than CLAUDE.md Part 6 but defensible for publishable-standards-strict gate.

  **Implications for Tier 4 Cowork Standards Briefing**:
   - The Cowork `hmtc-standards` skill must decide which gate it uses
   - **Strict gate (tooling READY)**: just 1 cell — fruit-juice-not-canned iAs (n=152 from FDA 2011 + FDA 2016)
   - **Permissive gate (master at-bar including summary-level corroborators)**: 47 cells documented; many with explicit summary-level caveats
   - Recommended for Standards Briefing: present BOTH tiers — strict-publishable (1 cell, no caveats) + curator-approved (50 cells with method/source caveats inline)

  **Defensibility check**: The master's at-bar cells remain defensible per CLAUDE.md Part 6 + Part 19 strict reading. The tooling adds a stricter sample-level-only filter as an extra gate. Neither layer should be silently abandoned — both serve different audiences (curator narrative vs operational queue).

  No stop conditions triggered. The curator-vs-tooling divergence is a documented architectural feature, not an unresolved schema decision.

  Tooling can be re-run any time via `node tools/evidence/build-standards-gap-report.mjs`. Adding new sample-level CSV files to `data/evidence/` (e.g., FDA TDS meat-subset, Brombach 2017 SI Table S1, Da Mato 2026 supplementary) would change the tooling's READY count.

## [2026-05-16] synthesis | organic-certification-not-protective — organic-vs-conventional heavy-metal pattern across 7 datasets
Pages touched: [[synthesis/organic-certification-not-protective]] (new), [[synthesis]] (index entry added), quartz.layout.ts (synthesis section exposed in sidebar Explorer for both single-page and list layouts).
Notes: Synthesis page established on 7 anchor sources spanning four continents and six matrices: Hands 2024 (US cocoa, organic Cd statistically higher), Brum 2025 (European flaxseed, organic Pb 652 vs 249 µg/kg conventional, p<0.05), Potortì 2026 (Italian-market tahini, organic Turkish T-1 the sole EU-exceedance outlier for both Cd and Pb), Słepecka 2017 (Polish cereals, ecological bran Cd 26.3 mg/kg vs undetectable conventional), Gardener 2019 (US infant foods n=564 CLP, p=0.65 Cd / p=0.28 Pb null), Grochowska-Niedworok 2020 (Polish tomatoes, organic higher dry-mass Cd), Dearing 2025 NZ (counterexample: organic LOWER Cd p=0.003 / Ni p<0.001 in post-Cyclone-Gabrielle NZ vegetables). Mechanistic case rests on (1) organic certification regulating inputs not occurrence outcomes; USDA NOP and EU 2018/848 silent on Pb/Cd/As/Hg; (2) single-origin organic sourcing increasing exposure to outlier soil-contamination regions; (3) organic fertilizers (manure, natural phosphates) often higher in Cd than EU-Cd-limited synthetic NPK; (4) lower-pH organic farm soils increasing Cd bioavailability. Sidebar fix: `synthesis` was in `hiddenPrimary` set in quartz.layout.ts for both defaultContentPageLayout and defaultListPageLayout, preventing the Synthesis dropdown from appearing in the Index Explorer. Removed both occurrences. Strategic implications drafted across brand-legal, regulator, consumer, and HMTc-relationship dimensions. Provisional status pending Consumer Reports 2023 chocolate ingest, HBBF follow-up datasets, and EU Fertilising Products Regulation Cd-limit derivation literature.

## [2026-05-16] synthesis | three pending candidates promoted — seaweed Cd/As, mining-impacted freshwater fish, climate-fish-MeHg
Pages touched: [[synthesis/seaweed-cd-as-contamination]] (new), [[synthesis/mining-impacted-freshwater-fish]] (new), [[synthesis/climate-fish-mehg-projections]] (new), [[synthesis]] (three index entries added), [[../../data/evidence/synthesis-proposals|synthesis-proposals]] (three entries moved to Promoted, pending entries struck through).
Notes: Worked the pending-candidate backlog in [data/evidence/synthesis-proposals.md](data/evidence/synthesis-proposals.md). Three of six pending threads were promotable; three remain pending (jarosite co-mitigation at 1/4, dietary-supplement Pb at 1/4, Egypt regulatory baseline explicitly recommended not-to-promote). Promoted: (1) seaweed-cd-as-contamination — three anchors (Nepper-Davidsen 2023, Hahn 2022, EFSA CONTAM 2023), criterion-1-through-4 met; biological-mechanism case via brown-algae alginate Cd-binding and arsenosugar As-metabolism, accumulation factors 10⁵-10⁶ from typical coastal seawater; complementary to organoarsenical-inertness synthesis. (2) mining-impacted-freshwater-fish — ten anchors across six continents and three contamination drivers (ASGM: Fuentes-López 2025 Colombia n=326, Lucchini 2025 Amazon, Shumba 2025 Zambia; industrial: Shaalan 2024 Egypt n=120 Pb 33.4/Cd 2.3/Hg 10.4 mg/kg in Nile tilapia; legacy: Barquero 2024 Almadén Spain bass tHg 7.61 mg/kg; impoundment: Willacker 2023 Snake River, Leclerc 2023 Québec run-of-river; US baseline: Stahl 2023 EPA NRSA, Lepak 2023 Colorado; mechanism: Zhou 2025 paddy microbial-methylation); multi-metal criterion closed by Shaalan 2024; complements established subsistence-fishing-mehg-disparity synthesis as the supply-side pair. (3) climate-fish-mehg-projections — three PNAS-level anchors (Sonke 2023 Ambio review + $117B economic-cost framing + 10-30% projection; Wang 2023 PNAS coupled-model RCP8.5 +5-20% by 2100; Motta 2022 PNAS Hg-isotope mechanism locating methylation in upper-400m marine particles); promotion is borderline on criterion 1 (review+projection+mechanism rather than three independent projections), synthesis page explicitly flagged as most provisional of the 2026-05-16 batch. Total wiki synthesis page count now 10 (was 6 before today's batch of 4 pages). Several new ingredient/product page targets surfaced as downstream consequences: [[ingredients/kelp]], [[ingredients/laver]], [[ingredients/kombu]], [[ingredients/nori]], [[ingredients/wakame]], [[ingredients/freshwater-fish]] (currently stub), [[ingredients/tilapia]], [[ingredients/freshwater-bass]], [[ingredients/tuna]], [[ingredients/swordfish]], [[ingredients/marlin]], [[ingredients/mackerel]], [[products/seaweed-snacks]], [[products/freshwater-fish]], [[products/canned-tuna]], [[mitigation/watershed-source-screening]]; these are downstream wiki work to be scoped separately. Synthesis backlog remaining: jarosite co-mitigation (1/4, awaiting 2 corroborating studies), dietary-supplement Pb (1/4, awaiting 2 corroborating A/B-tier studies), Egypt regulatory baseline (do-not-promote, methodology-note territory).
